# Krator All-51 Settlements (`60`-Target) Master Audit: Scale Model `5.1` Terrain Fit, Pin Relocations, Map Surgery & Continental-Scope Rebuild Blueprint (`Rulings #1–114`)

> **Machine-Measured Baseline:** Audited directly against `tools/scale-model/model/data/` (`5.1e21`–`5.1e23`, `1821 × 1723` at `2.0 km/px`, `lo = -2,600 m`, `hi = +27,000 m`), `rivers.json` (`896`+`32` Ria courses), `canals.json` (`22` lock/barge canals), `basins.json` (`178` lakes), `landforms.json`, `tubes.json`, `springs.json`, `vents.json`, `60-settlements.js`, `51-region-store.js`, `openworld/SITES.json`, `kits/resources/data/geology.json` (`911 × 862` at `4.0 km/cell`, `39` resource/rock/ledger layers), and all locked rulings (`Rulings #1–114`) across `core/region/*-SCOPE.md` (including Scopes `#7` [CONVOY-LOGISTICS-SCOPE.md](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/core/region/CONVOY-LOGISTICS-SCOPE.md), `#3` [CONTRABAND-LAW-PENAL-SCOPE.md](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/core/region/CONTRABAND-LAW-PENAL-SCOPE.md), and `#2` [ESPIONAGE-DIPLOMACY-SCOPE.md](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/core/region/ESPIONAGE-DIPLOMACY-SCOPE.md)).
> **Raw Machine Dataset:** `scratch/settlements_audit_raw.json`

---

## 0. Executive Summary & Prescription Counts

- **Total Settlement Folders Audited:** **`51`** (`60` explicit targets including multi-target sites in `isle-demo` [`Kautoa`, `Valapo`, `Nephtos`], `iziz` [`Iziz City/Palace`, `Pylon`, `Talia`], `highlands` [`Roketstad` + kit sheets], `xanadu` [`Erewhon` + kit host], `azure-dream` [`Monastery`, `Summit`, `3` villages], and `gihon` [`Gihon`, `The Landing`]).
- **Prescription Breakdown Across the `51` Settlement Folders (`60` Targets):**
  - **`[A]` Direct 1:1 `WORLD.H` Fit + `KCITY` Rebuild Blueprint:** **`18` folders** (`24` targets)
  - **`[B]` Relocate Pin to Nearby Optimal Site `(px_new, py_new)` + Rebuild Blueprint:** **`7` folders** (`7` targets)
  - **`[C]` Sub-Pixel Landform Stamp (`landforms.json` / `KSTACKS` / `KCARVE` / `KCAVERN` / `WORLD.landHook`) — No Macro `h.png` Surgery:** **`22` folders** (`25` targets)
  - **`[D]` Targeted Scale-Model `5.1` Map Surgery (`edit_522_*.py`):** **`4` folders** (`4` targets)

### Key Audit Discoveries on Live `5.1e21` Rasters (`h.png` / `wl.png` / `rivers.json`)
1. **Prior Map Surgeries Verified Already Baked on Disk:** `edit_513_voth.py` (`Voth` bay + `Turm`/`Vindos` rivers), `edit_517*.py` (`Erewhon` narrowed caldera lake + `Phēr` headland + `Azure Dream` Great Isle), `edit_520_republic_alps.py` (`Roketstad` `L1 Roketsjö` + `Snou Bruk` gorge + alpine massifs), and `edit_521_wave0_hygiene.py` (`+900 m` East Abyss floor lift at `Mungo` `-1,433 m` & `Reed` `-1,750 m`) **are already live in `h.png` / `wl.png`** — do NOT re-run them.
2. **Four Critical Submerged / Misplaced Pins Solved Cleanly by `[B]` Pin Relocations (Zero Map Surgery):**
   - **`shim` (`654, 1118` -> `655, 1111`):** Current pin is **submerged `452 m` deep in Great SW Lake** (`h = -461.4 m`, `wet = True`, `wlev = -9.2 m`), `17.8 km` S of `Shim/klir Rivrh`! Moving `7.1 px` (`14.2 km`) N to **`(655, 1111)`** (`+9.0 m` dry shore) places Shim right between the `-9.2 m` chinampa embayment (`2 km` W) and the `5,601 m³/s` `Shim/klir Rivrh` (`4 km` N)!
   - **`zeydanin` (`795, 860` -> `792, 860` or `793, 855`):** Current pin sits **`7.2 km` inland at `-1,180 m` (`+320 m` up the Throne flank above the `-1,500 m` Ring Sea)**, stranding its fleet harbour, Hykkousoi sea-mole, and bireme pens! Moving `3.0 px` (`6.0 km`) W to **`(792, 860)`** (`-1,444 m` dry shore) or `5.4 px` NW to sheltered bay **`(793, 855)`** (`-1,492 m`) restores coastal access immediately.
   - **`gihon` (`955, 1607` -> `955, 1608`):** Unpinned `ll.opal-mouth` `(955, 1607)` in `gihon/PLAN.md` & `xan-tune.js` is a **wet lake pixel** (`h = -1,710.2 m`, `25.3 m` under `-1,684.9 m` Lower Xanadu Lake)! Snapping `1 px` (`2 km`) S to **`(955, 1608)`** (`-1,614 m` dry shore) puts the 8,500-pop Mint & Golden Tears fortress on dry ground at the `115 m³/s` Opal River mouth.
   - **`qoold-lheek` (`410, 506` -> `411.4, 508.1`):** `60-settlements.js` (`s427qb1kma`) still points at the `+6,030 m` exposed cliff rim (`350 m` sheer drop above `+5,680 m` Cold Lake), whereas `qoold-lheek/src/30-ql-layout.js` already uses the thaw-stable coulee gravel fan at **`(411.4, 508.1)`** (`+5,702 m` shore + `Musïq Quli` stream + high gold/tin placers).
   - **Plus 3 River-Alignment `[B]` Moves:** **`ardent`** `(830, 553)` (`22.6 km` from any stream) -> **`(819, 552)`** (on `r024 Mēkam-āṟu`, `16.8 m³/s`); **`drum`** `(673, 932)` (`+317 m` up valley wall) -> **`(671, 930)`** (valley floor on `r058 Drïm Rivrh`, `49.9 m³/s`); **`torus`** `(774, 1007)` (`9.7 km` off river near coastal cliff) -> **`(777, 1003)`** (on `r072 Tors Rivrh`, `59.3 m³/s`).
3. **Only Four Surgical `[D]` Map Edits (`edit_522_*.py`) Needed Continent-Wide (Disjoint Boxes):**
   - **`almua` (`edit_522_almua_dip.py`):** Current `(718, 883)` sits **`9 km` offshore in `513 m` deep Ring Sea** (`-2,013.3 m`), AND `r863 Dip Rivrh` (`38.3 m³/s`) has broken `5.1` vertices ending `76 km` SW at `[688.5, 910.8]` while its `to.at_px` still says `[720.6, 876.8]`! Snap pin to dry N-facing shore **`(723, 885)`** (`-1,442 m`) and route `Dip Rivrh` into a `2-px` spit harbour there (or `[B]` zero-surgery fallback `(699, 890)` at `Kowld Brük` mouth).
   - **`beacon` (`edit_522_beacon_knoll.py`):** On `main`'s `h.png`, `(853, 1540)` is **`+323.6 m`** (lowered by `edit_517` lake re-run — the `+863 m` knoll restoration in `beacon/PLAN.md` never landed on `main`!), blocking sightlines to `Tuatara`, `Tempest`, and `march.s`. Restore the `6 × 10 px` oval knoll around `(854, 1540)` (`+863..+885 m` crown + `+849 m` saddle) and add pin `sbeacon00001`.
   - **`dalab` (`edit_522_dalab_delta.py`):** Owner-requested `Wind Bruk` delta-plain grading (`473..480 × 1105..1111`) at `(477, 1108)` to flatten `293 m` of `5×5` hill relief into a `+4..+14 m` alluvial shelf and fill the `-12 m` dry pit at `(477, 1107)`.
   - **`locus` (`edit_522_locus_stream.py`):** Add a short `18 km` perennial scarp-foot stream (`r_locus_delta`, `~15 m³/s`) off the eastern wall into `(1267, 1043)` so Locus's 3-arm marsh delta has a real `rivers.json` inflow (`d_river` is currently `40.4 km`).
4. **Unpinned Settlements to Register in `60-settlements.js` (`SETT_ADDED['5.1e22']`):** `eternian-canal` (*Shuāng-Zhá*, `450.5, 782.0`), `blakhed` (`441, 1400`), `beacon` (`853, 1540`), `flashing-eye` (`881, 1527.1`), `viridian` (`962, 1562`), `iridescent` (`1016, 1655`), `gihon` (`955, 1608`), `gihon/landing` (`932, 1600`), and `devisers-camp` (`839, 1398`). (`iron-millstream` stays an unpinned hydropower sub-site of `Roketstad`; `azure-dream` sub-sites belong to `Grēṭ-Āil`.)
